<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - object)
    (cats ?o - object)
    (texture ?o - object)
    (vase ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    (next ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    (sneeze ?o - object)
    (spring ?o - object)
    (stupendous ?o - object)
    (collect ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object ?o3 - object)
    :precondition (and (sneeze ?o0) (texture ?o1) (texture ?o2) (stupendous ?o3) (next ?o0 ?o1) (collect ?o1 ?o3) (collect ?o2 ?o3))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (cats ?o0) (spring ?o1) (spring ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )
)
</domain>

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - object)
    (cats ?o - object)
    (texture ?o - object)
    (vase ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    (next ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    (sneeze ?o - object)
    (spring ?o - object)
    (stupendous ?o - object)
    (collect ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object ?o3 - object)
    :precondition (and (sneeze ?o0) (texture ?o1) (texture ?o2) (stupendous ?o3) (next ?o0 ?o1) (collect ?o1 ?o3) (collect ?o2 ?o3))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (cats ?o0) (spring ?o1) (spring ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )
)

**Step 1: Perform the Sip action**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:00 - 00:01
*   **Action:** Use **object_8** (hand), **object_1** (cats), and **object_7** (texture).
*   **Description:** By performing this action, you utilize the existing connections (where both the hand and the cat are next to the texture object_7) to establish a "vase" relationship between the hand (object_8) and the cat (object_1). 
*   **Result:** A vase relationship is formed between object_8 and object_1; object_8 is no longer "next" to object_7.

**Step 2: Perform the Memory action**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:01 - 00:02
*   **Action:** Use **object_1** (cats), **object_7** (spring), and **object_6** (spring).
*   **Description:** This action transitions the cat's connection. Since the cat (object_1) was previously next to spring object_7, you now shift that connection so it is next to spring object_6.
*   **Result:** Object_1 is now "next" to object_6; the connection between object_1 and object_7 is severed.

**Step 3: Perform the Paltry action**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:02 - 00:03
*   **Action:** Use **object_8** (hand), **object_1** (cats), and **object_6** (texture).
*   **Description:** With the cat (object_1) now positioned next to the texture object_6 and the vase relationship between the hand and cat still active, you can perform the paltry action to finalize the routing.
*   **Result:** The goal is achieved: **object_8 is now next to object_6**. The vase relationship between object_8 and object_1 is removed.

*   **Result:** **object_0** is now "next" to **object_6**.

**Step 4: Execute Paltry Action (Timestamp: 00:03)**

- **Participants:** object_9 (hand), object_4 (sneeze), and object_7 (texture).
- **Timestamp:** T+0:00
- **Description:** Begin by performing the **clip** action. This uses the hand (object_9) to interact with a sneeze (object_4) and a specific texture (object_7). This action links object_9 and object_4 in a "vase" relationship while removing the existing "next" connection between object_9 and object_7.

**Step 2: Execute the wretched process on object_4, object_7, object_8, and object_2**
- **Action:** wretched
- **Participants:** object_4 (sneeze), object_7 (texture), object_8 (texture), and object_2 (stupendous).
- **Timestamp:** T+0:05
- **Description:** Perform the **wretched** action. This involves the sneeze (object_4) transitioning its connection from one texture (object_7) to another (object_8). This process is facilitated by the stupendous item (object_2), as both textures are designated to be collected by it. Upon completion, object_4 is now "next" to object_8.

**Step 3: Apply the tightfisted action to object_9, object_4, and object_8**
- **Action:** tightfisted
- **Participants:** object_9 (hand), object_4 (sneeze), and object_8 (texture).
- **Timestamp:** T+0:10
- **Description:** Finally, perform the **tightfisted** action. Using the hand (object_9), the previous "vase" connection with the sneeze (object_4) is leveraged alongside the sneeze's new proximity to the texture (object_8). This completes the sequence, establishing the goal state: **next object_9 object_8**.
